[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `context/starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];
      end
      // the PE mesh
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe
              (.clk        (clk),
               .rst        (rst),
               .ld         (ld),
               .en         (en),
               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),
               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),
               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),
               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),
               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
Baseline passes with cost 4,624,409 (area 4604, delay 1004 ps — the 32-bit accumulator chain is slow). Key insight: the true dynamic range only needs 19 signed bits (max |Σ of 8 products| = 8·128·128 = 2^17), so I can shrink the internal accumulator from 32→19 bits and sign-extend only at the output. Let me create that variant.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Optimized variant 1: internal accumulator narrowed to 19 signed bits.\n//\n// Max |partial sum| over 8 products of 8x8 signed = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17,\n// which fits exactly in 19 signed bits.  The 32-bit width is restored only\n// at the output port by sign extension (pure wiring).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // internal accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t      // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north +\n                          {{(IW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*IW +: IW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*IW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, in narrow width.\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*IW +: IW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-IW){cin[IW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {IW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-IW){dr[D-1][IW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design1.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The 19-bit accumulator was a solid win. Now the big structural optimization: the critical path is the multiplier + 19-bit carry-propagate adder inside each PE. I'll switch the partial-sum chain to **carry-save (redundant) form**: each PE generates raw multiplier partial-product rows and compresses them with the incoming carry-save pair via a CSA tree — no carry-propagate adder anywhere in the array. Conversion to binary happens once per column, after the de-skew chain.

Math check: max |Σ products| = 8·16384 = 2^17 exactly, so 19-bit two's complement (mod 2^19) suffices; all CS arithmetic is mod 2^19 with sign-extended operands.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Optimized variant 2: carry-save accumulation.\n//\n// The partial sums flowing down each column are kept in carry-save (redundant)\n// form: two IW-bit vectors (S, C) with S + C == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which fits in 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Each PE multiplies by building raw partial-product rows (no carry-propagate\n// adder) and compresses them together with the incoming (S, C) pair using a\n// Wallace-style tree of 3:2 compressors.  The carry-propagate conversion to\n// binary happens exactly once per column, after the output de-skew chain, so\n// it is off the systolic critical path.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + c == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t      // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  c_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  c_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Partial products of the signed product a_west * w, as plain bit rows\n   // (mod 2^IW).  With au = a_west interpreted unsigned and wu = w unsigned:\n   //   a*w = au*wu - 2^8*au*w[7] - 2^8*wu*a[7] + 2^16*a[7]*w[7]\n   // Negated rows are added as their bitwise complement plus a single +1\n   // correction bit at the row's LSB position (standard two's-complement\n   // negation of an addend).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] asext = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] wsext = {{(IW-DW){w[DW-1]}}, w};\n   wire [IW-1:0] negA  = ~asext;\n   wire [IW-1:0] negW  = ~wsext;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] row [0:12];\n\n   // unsigned product rows: (w & {8{a[i]}}) << i, i = 0..7\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < DW; i = i + 1) begin : g_pp\n         assign row[i] = (w & {DW{a_west[i]}}) << i;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   assign row[8]  = w[DW-1]   ? (negA << (DW-0)) : {IW{1'b0}}; // -au << 8\n   assign row[9]  = a_west[DW-1] ? (negW << (DW-0)) : {IW{1'b0}}; // -wu << 8\n   assign row[10] = w[DW-1]   << DW;\t\t// +1 << 8 (negation corr.)\n   assign row[11] = a_west[DW-1] << DW;\t\t// +1 << 8 (negation corr.)\n   assign row[12] = (a_west[DW-1] & w[DW-1]) << (2*DW); // +2^16\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Wallace-style compression of 13 rows (S, C, 11 product rows) down to 2.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] s1_0, c1_0, s1_1, c1_1, s1_2, c1_2, s1_3, c1_3;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l1_0 (.x(row[0]),  .y(row[1]),  .z(row[2]),\n\t\t\t  .s(s1_0), .c(c1_0));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l1_1 (.x(row[3]),  .y(row[4]),  .z(row[5]),\n\t\t\t  .s(s1_1), .c(c1_1));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l1_2 (.x(row[6]),  .y(row[7]),  .z(row[8]),\n\t\t\t  .s(s1_2), .c(c1_2));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l1_3 (.x(row[9]),  .y(row[10]), .z(row[11]),\n\t\t\t  .s(s1_3), .c(c1_3));\n   // row[12] passes to the next level\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s2_0, c2_0, s2_1, c2_1, s2_2, c2_2;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l2_0 (.x(s1_0), .y(c1_0), .z(s1_1), .s(s2_0), .c(c2_0));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l2_1 (.x(c1_1), .y(s1_2), .z(c1_2), .s(s2_1), .c(c2_1));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l2_2 (.x(s1_3), .y(c1_3), .z(row[12]),\n\t\t\t  .s(s2_2), .c(c2_2));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s3_0, c3_0, s3_1, c3_1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l3_0 (.x(s2_0), .y(c2_0), .z(s2_1), .s(s3_0), .c(c3_0));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l3_1 (.x(c2_1), .y(s2_2), .z(c2_2), .s(s3_1), .c(c3_1));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s4_0, c4_0;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l4_0 (.x(s3_0), .y(c3_0), .z(s3_1), .s(s4_0), .c(c4_0));\n   // c3_1 passes to the last level\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s5_0, c5_0;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_l5_0 (.x(s4_0), .y(c4_0), .z(c3_1),\n\t\t\t  .s(s5_0), .c(c5_0));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w       <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         s_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n         c_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east  <= a_west;\n            s_south <= s5_0;\n            c_south <= c5_0;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\ts_bot,\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0] c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign s_bot[j*IW +: IW] = s_v[N][j];\n         assign c_bot[j*IW +: IW] = c_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north    (s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north    (c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south    (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south    (c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*IW-1:0] s_bot, c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .s_bot(s_bot), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles in carry-save form; the\n   // carry-propagate conversion to binary happens after the de-skew chain.\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] sin = s_bot[j*IW +: IW];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*IW +: IW];\n         reg [IW-1:0] sr [0:D-1];\n         reg [IW-1:0] cr [0:D-1];\n         integer k;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1) begin\n                  sr[k] <= {IW{1'b0}};\n                  cr[k] <= {IW{1'b0}};\n               end\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               sr[0] <= sin;\n               cr[0] <= cin;\n               for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) begin\n                  sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n                  cr[k] <= cr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n         wire [IW-1:0] tf = sr[D-1] + cr[D-1];\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                {{(ACCW-IW){tf[IW-1]}}, tf};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
Found it — a classic partial-product bug: I complemented the **sign-extended** operand instead of the **zero-extended** one. The negated rows must be the 11-bit complement of the unsigned operand (+1 at bit 8). Fixing:
  -> edit_file(filename='design2.v', new_string="   //##########################################################################\n   // Partial products of the signed product a_west * w, as plain bit rows\n   // (mod 2^IW).  With au = a_west interpreted unsigned and wu = w unsigned:\n   //   a*w = au*wu - 2^8*au*w[7] - 2^8*wu*a[7] + 2^16*a[7]*w[7]\n   // A negated unsigned operand x (as an 11-bit quantity) is added as its\n   // 11-bit bitwise complement plus a single +1 correction bit at the row's\n   // LSB position (bit 8 here), i.e. (~x + 1) << 8 = -x << 8 (mod 2^IW).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [10:0] negA11 = {3'b111, ~a_west};   // 11-bit compl. of au\n   wire [10:0] negW11 = {3'b111, ~w};\t   // 11-bit compl. of wu\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] row [0:12];\n\n   // unsigned product rows: (w & {8{a[i]}}) << i, i = 0..7\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < DW; i = i + 1) begin : g_pp\n         assign row[i] = (w & {DW{a_west[i]}}) << i;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   assign row[8]  = w[DW-1]      ? ({{(IW-11){1'b0}}, negA11} << DW)\n\t\t\t\t : {IW{1'b0}};\t\t// -au << 8\n   assign row[9]  = a_west[DW-1] ? ({{(IW-11){1'b0}}, negW11} << DW)\n\t\t\t\t : {IW{1'b0}};\t\t// -wu << 8\n   assign row[10] = {9'b0, w[DW-1], 8'b0};\t// +1 << 8 (negation corr.)\n   assign row[11] = {9'b0, a_west[DW-1], 8'b0};\t// +1 << 8 (negation corr.)\n   assign row[12] = (a_west[DW-1] & w[DW-1]) << (2*DW); // +2^16", old_string="   //##########################################################################\n   // Partial products of the signed product a_west * w, as plain bit rows\n   // (mod 2^IW).  With au = a_west interpreted unsigned and wu = w unsigned:\n   //   a*w = au*wu - 2^8*au*w[7] - 2^8*wu*a[7] + 2^16*a[7]*w[7]\n   // Negated rows are added as their bitwise complement plus a single +1\n   // correction bit at the row's LSB position (standard two's-complement\n   // negation of an addend).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] asext = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] wsext = {{(IW-DW){w[DW-1]}}, w};\n   wire [IW-1:0] negA  = ~asext;\n   wire [IW-1:0] negW  = ~wsext;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] row [0:12];\n\n   // unsigned product rows: (w & {8{a[i]}}) << i, i = 0..7\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < DW; i = i + 1) begin : g_pp\n         assign row[i] = (w & {DW{a_west[i]}}) << i;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   assign row[8]  = w[DW-1]   ? (negA << (DW-0)) : {IW{1'b0}}; // -au << 8\n   assign row[9]  = a_west[DW-1] ? (negW << (DW-0)) : {IW{1'b0}}; // -wu << 8\n   assign row[10] = w[DW-1]   << DW;\t\t// +1 << 8 (negation corr.)\n   assign row[11] = a_west[DW-1] << DW;\t\t// +1 << 8 (negation corr.)\n   assign row[12] = (a_west[DW-1] & w[DW-1]) << (2*DW); // +2^16")

  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Optimized variant 3: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth encoding.\n//\n// The partial sums flowing down each column are kept in carry-save (redundant)\n// form: two IW-bit vectors (S, C) with S + C == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Each PE multiplies with radix-4 Booth recoding (4 partial-product rows for\n// an 8-bit operand) and merges the rows with the incoming (S, C) pair in a\n// 5-compressor tree.  The carry-propagate conversion to binary happens once\n// per column, after the output de-skew chain, off the systolic path.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + c == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t      // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  c_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  c_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Radix-4 Booth partial products: a*w = sum_i d_i * w * 4^i, i = 0..3,\n   // d_i in {-2,-1,0,1,2} decoded from (a[2i+1], a[2i], a[2i-1]) (a[-1]=0).\n   // Each row is the 10-bit signed value |d_i|*w, conditionally negated\n   // (~x + 1, the +1 injected as a correction bit at column 2i) and\n   // sign-extended to IW bits, then shifted into position.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [3:0] neg, two, sel;\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_booth\n         wire hi  = a_west[2*i+1];\n         wire mid = a_west[2*i];\n         wire lo  = (i == 0) ? 1'b0 : a_west[2*i-1];\n         assign neg[i] = hi & ~(mid & lo);\n         assign two[i] = (hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo);\n         assign sel[i] = (hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo);\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // magnitude row x (10-bit signed): two ? 2w : w\n   wire [9:0] x;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 8; k = k + 1) begin : g_x\n         assign x[k] = two[0] ? ((k == 0) ? 1'b0 : w[k-1]) : w[k];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign x[8] = w[DW-1];\n   assign x[9] = w[DW-1];\n\n   // Booth rows, sign-extended to IW bits\n   wire [9:0] rp [0:3];\n   wire [3:0] ext;\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rpb\n            assign rp[i][k] = sel[i] & (neg[i] ^ x[k]);\n         end\n         assign ext[i] = rp[i][9];\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){ext[i]}}, rp[i]} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // negation correction bits (+1) at columns 0, 2, 4, 6\n   wire [3:0] corr;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_corr\n         assign corr[i] = sel[i] & neg[i];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // absorb corr[0..2] into unused low slots of rows 1..3; corr[3] stands alone\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1c = brow[1] | (corr[0] << 0);\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2c = brow[2] | (corr[1] << 2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3c = brow[3] | (corr[2] << 4);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c3v = corr[3] << 6;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Compression of 7 rows (B0, B1', B2', B3', corr3, S, C) down to 2:\n   // 5 compressors, 4 levels.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0, u0c, u1, u1c;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_a (.x(brow[0]), .y(b1c),    .z(b2c),    .s(u0), .c(u0c));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_b (.x(b3c),     .y(c3v),    .z(s_north), .s(u1), .c(u1c));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] v0, v1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_c (.x(u0), .y(u0c), .z(u1), .s(f0), .c(f1));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w       <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         s_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n         c_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east  <= a_west;\n            s_south <= f0;\n            c_south <= f1;\n 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i Key insight: column *j*'s bottom partial sum is available at cycle m+8+*j* while c_valid comes at m+15 — so instead of delay chains, I can capture each column's converted result into a single 8×19 output register, enabled by `a_valid` delayed 8+*j* cycles.   -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 4: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth + capture-register\n// output alignment (no de-skew shift chains) + async reset.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// two IW-bit vectors (S, C) with S + C == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Output alignment: the bottom of column j holds A-row m's partial sum at\n// cycle m+8+j; c_valid for row m is at m+15.  So each column's carry-save\n// pair is converted to binary (one 19-bit CPA) and captured into a shared\n// output register when a_valid, delayed by 8+j cycles, is high.  The\n// register is guaranteed to hold row m's value at cycle m+15 (the next\n// capture of column j happens 8 cycles later).  This replaces the classic\n// per-column de-skew shift chains with 8x19 capture registers.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + c == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t      // asynchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  c_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  c_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Radix-4 Booth partial products: a*w = sum_i d_i * w * 4^i, i = 0..3,\n   // d_i = -2*a[2i+1] + a[2i] + a[2i-1] in {-2..2} (a[-1] = 0).\n   // Each row is the 10-bit signed magnitude |d_i|*w, conditionally\n   // complemented (~x; the matching +1 is injected as a correction bit at\n   // column 2i), then sign-extended to IW bits and shifted into position.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [3:0] neg, two, sel;\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_booth\n         wire hi  = a_west[2*i+1];\n         wire mid = a_west[2*i];\n         wire lo  = (i == 0) ? 1'b0 : a_west[2*i-1];\n         assign neg[i] = hi & ~(mid & lo);\n         assign two[i] = (hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo);\n         assign sel[i] = (hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo);\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Booth rows: per-row magnitude (|d_i| * w as 10-bit signed value),\n   // conditionally negated (~x; the sign extension of ~x plus the +1\n   // correction bit at column 2i equals -x), then sign-extended to IW bits\n   // and shifted into position.\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] x;\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         wire\t ei;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_mg\n            // magnitude: two ? 2w : w  (10-bit signed)\n            assign x[k] = (k == 0) ? (two[i] ? 1'b0 : w[0]) :\n\t\t\t  ((k >= 1) && (k <= 7)) ?\n\t\t\t\t\t(two[i] ? w[k-1] : w[k]) :\n\t\t\t  w[DW-1];\t// k == 8 or 9: sign bit\n            // conditional negation (~x), applied only when selected\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] & (neg[i] ^ x[k]);\n         end\n         assign ei = rp[9];\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){ei}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n [ASSISTANT]
Design3 (Booth+CSA) passes at 2.62M. Now design5 with several refinements: (1) carry vector stored unshifted — its bit 0 is structurally always 0, so store only 18 bits (saves 64 FFs); (2) convert CS→binary at each column bottom, then de-skew in binary (532 FFs instead of 1064); (3) drop reset from all datapath FFs — accumulations always start at the tied-zero top row, and skew/de-skew garbage only flows into never-output rows (Verilator zero-inits anyway), leaving reset only on the valid pipe.

[ASSISTANT]
Design6 failed for two reasons I've now verified: (1) **IW=18 is impossible** — max positive sum +131072 = 2^17 needs 19 signed bits (18-bit signed max is +131071); (2) my "fused" Booth identity was algebraically wrong. Design5 (2.50M) stands as best.

Next big idea: **Booth-encode the stationary weight instead of the live activation**. The digits (sel/neg/t) get decoded once at load time (8 top decoders instead of 64 in-PE decoders) and shift down as 12-bit control chains. Per cycle, each partial-product row is just a **shifted copy of ±a** — the Booth decode disappears entirely from the a_west→CSA critical path (~3-4 gate levels saved):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 7: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth recoding of the\n// STATIONARY weight (controls pre-decoded at load, 12-bit control chain).\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0 (majority of operands with a structural zero) and\n// G's bit IW-1 is irrelevant mod 2^IW (it is used shifted), so only\n// G[IW-2:0] (18 bits) is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Booth recoding of w (stationary): w = sum_i d_i * 4^i, d_i in {-2..2},\n// decoded ONCE at load time into per-digit controls (sel, neg, t).  Per\n// compute cycle the partial-product rows are shifted copies of the live\n// activation: row i = (±a) << (2*i + t_i) with sign chosen by neg_i, so the\n// Booth decode is OFF the a_west -> CSA critical path.  Negation (+1) is\n// injected as correction bits absorbed into the zero region below the next\n// row's LSB (corr_3 forms a 1-bit sparse row).\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t   clk,\n    input\t\t   ld,\t   // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t   en,\t   // compute enable\n    input [11:0]\t   wc_north, // Booth controls from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t   a_west,  // activation in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t   s_north, // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t   g_north, // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [11:0]\t   wc_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t   a_east,  // activation out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t   s_south, // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t   g_south  // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [11:0] wc;\n   assign wc_south = wc;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Partial-product rows from the pre-decoded Booth controls of w:\n   // row i = sel_i * (-1)^neg_i * a << (2*i + t_i)   (a = live activation)\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0] a = a_west;\n\n   genvar i, k;\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n\t wire\t   sel_i = wc[3*i+2];\n\t wire\t   neg_i = wc[3*i+1];\n\t wire\t   t_i   = wc[3*i];\n\t wire\t   corr_i = sel_i & neg_i;\n\t // 10-bit signed a: {a[7], a[7], a[7:0]}\n\t wire [9:0] rp;\n\t for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n\t    wire [DW-1:0] a10 =\n\t\t  {(k >= 8) ? a[DW-1] : a[k], a};  // placeholder, replaced below\n\t    assign rp[k] = corr_i ^ (sel_i &\n\t\t\t\t     ((k >= 8) ? a[DW-1] : a[k]));\n\t end\n\t wire ext_i = rp[9];\n\t // dynamic extra shift by t_i, with sign extension\n\t wire [10:0] sv;\n\t assign sv[0] = t_i ? 1'b0 : rp[0];\n\t for (k = 1; k <= 9; k = k + 1) begin : g_sv\n\t    assign sv[k] = t_i ? rp[k-1] : rp[k];\n\t end\n\t assign sv[10] = rp[9];\n\n\t wire [IW-1:0] brow_i;\n\t assign brow_i[2*i +: 11] = sv;\n\t if (i < 3) begin : g_ext\n\t    assign brow_i[18 : 2*i+11] = {(8-2*i){ext_i}};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_ext3\n\t    assign brow_i[18 : 17] = {ext_i, ext_i};\n\t end\n\t assign brow[i] = brow_i;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // negation correction bits (+1) at columns 2i + t_i, absorbed into the\n   // zero region below row i+1's LSB (row i+1 starts at column 2i+2 or more)\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1c = brow[1] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr0_lo}),\n\t\t  b2c = brow[2] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr1_lo}),\n\t\t  b3c = brow[3] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr2_lo});\n   // (see assignments below for corr0_lo/1/2)\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        wc <= wc_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         s_south <= f0;\n         g_south <= f1[IW-2:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design7.v')

  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 7: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth recoding of the\n// STATIONARY weight (controls pre-decoded at load; 12-bit control chain\n// shifts south with the weight chain).\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Booth recoding of w (stationary): w = sum_i d_i * 4^i, d_i in {-2..2},\n// decoded ONCE at load time into per-digit controls (sel, neg, t).  Per\n// compute cycle partial-product row i is a shifted copy of the live\n// activation: row i = sel_i * (-1)^neg_i * a << (2i + t_i), so the Booth\n// decode sits OFF the a_west -> CSA critical path (a shift/mux only).\n// Negation (+1) correction bits land at columns 2i+t_i, inside the zero\n// padding below row i+1's LSB, so corr[0..2] are ORed into rows 1..3 for\n// free; [ASSISTANT]
Design7's control-chain idea is architecturally right but I introduced an all-ones bug in the correction OR (`{IW{corr}}` instead of a single bit). More importantly, I realize the *activation-side* Booth decode can be hoisted out of all 64 PEs: each PE's product rows depend on the activation **only through the 12 digit-control bits** (sel/neg/two), so I can decode once per row at the west edge and pipe 12-bit controls east instead of 8-bit activation — removing the decode from the per-PE critical path entirely.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 8: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth with the activation\n// digit decode hoisted OUT of the PE array.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Radix-4 Booth on the ACTIVATION: a*w = sum_i d_i * w * 4^i with digits\n// d_i in {-2..2} decoded from a[2i+1], a[2i], a[2i-1].  The product rows\n// depend on the activation ONLY through the per-digit controls\n// (sel, neg, two), so the decode is done ONCE per row (8 decoders instead\n// of 64) at the row's west edge, and a 12-bit control vector is piped east\n// through the array (replacing the 8-bit activation chain).  Inside a PE\n// the row generation is then just a w-side mux (w is stationary/registered)\n// plus one XOR and one AND - off the deep path.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [11:0]\t  c_west,     // Booth digit controls in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [11:0]\t  c_east,     // Booth digit controls out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0\n   wire [3:0] sel = {c_west[11], c_west[8], c_west[5], c_west[2]};\n   wire [3:0] neg = {c_west[10], c_west[7], c_west[4], c_west[1]};\n   wire [3:0] two = {c_west[9],  c_west[6], c_west[3], c_west[0]};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (-1)^neg_i * (two_i ? 2w : w) << (2*i),\n   // as 10-bit signed magnitudes sign-extended to IW bits.  The magnitude\n   // mux runs on the stationary weight only.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n            wire xk = (k == 0) ? (two[i] ? 1'b0 : w[0]) :\n\t\t      ((k >= 1) && (k <= 7)) ?\n\t\t\t\t\t(two[i] ? w[k-1] : w[k]) :\n\t\t      w[DW-1];\t// k == 8 or 9: sign bit\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] & (neg[i] ^ xk);\n         end\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){rp[9]}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // negation correction bits (+1) at columns 0, 2, 4, 6\n   wire [3:0] corr;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_corr\n         assign corr[i] = sel[i] & neg[i];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // absorb corr[0..2] into unused low slots of rows 1..3; corr[3] stands alone\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1c = brow[1] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr[0]});\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2c = brow[2] | ({{(IW-2){1'b0}}, corr[1]} << 2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3c = brow[3] | ({{(IW-3){1'b0}}, corr[2]} << 4);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c3v = {{(IW-7){1'b0}}, corr[3]} << 6;\n\n   // incoming carry-save row (g is stored unshifted)\n   wire [IW-1:0] crow = {g_north, 1'b0};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Compression of 7 rows (B0, B1', B2', B3', corr3, S, C) down to 2:\n   // 5 compressors, 4 levels (minimum for 7 rows with 3:2 compressors).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0, u0g, u1, u1g;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_a (.x(brow[0]), .y(b1c),  .z(b2c),   .s(u0), .g(u0g));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_b (.x(b3c),     .y(c3v),  .z(s_north), .s(u1), .g(u1g));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] v0, v1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_c (.x(u0), .y({u0g[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(u1),\n\t\t       .s(v0), .g(v1));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] w0r, w1r;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_d (.x(v0), .y({v1[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z({u1g[IW-2:0], 1'b0}),\n\t\t       .s(w0r), .g(w1r));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] f0, f1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_e (.x(w0r), .y({w1r[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(crow),\n\t\t       .s(f0), .g(f1));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n        w       <= w_north;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         c_east  <= c_west;\n         s_south <= f0;\n         g_south <= f1[IW-2:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*12-1:0]\tc_left,   // per-row Booth digit controls\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\ts_bot,\n    output [N*(IW-1)-1:0] g_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [12-1:0] c_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-2:0] g_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign g_v[0][j] = {(IW-1){1'b0}};\n         .ld       (ld),\n               .en       (en),\n               .w_north  (w_v[i][j]),\n               .c_west   (c_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north  (s_v[i][j]),\n               .g_north  (g_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south  (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_east   (c_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south  (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .g_south  (g_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Booth digit decode, once per row (at the array's west edge):\n   // controls {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for digits i = 3..0.\n   wire [N*12-1:0] c_left;\n   genvar\t   q, d;\n   generate\n      for (q = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n         wire [DW-1:0] aq = a_left[q*DW +: DW];\n         for (d = 0; d < 4; d = d + 1) begin : g_d\n            wire hi  = aq[2*d+1];\n            wire mid = aq[2*d];\n            wire lo  = (d == 0) ? 1'b0 : aq[2*d-1];\n            assign c_left[q*12 + 3*d + 2] = (hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo);\n            assign c_left[q*12 +
