[ASSISTANT]
Design8 (2.4987M) only marginally beats design5. Now a bigger structural idea: **eliminate the eastward activation/control chains entirely**. Since PE(i,j) must process A-row m at cycle m+i+j, and the psum chain already provides the per-row stage delay, each PE can compute its product **combinationally from the row-broadcast skewed activation** in the same cycle it accumulates — no a_east registers at all (saves 224 FFs), and Booth controls decoded once at the input and skewed as 12-bit vectors (no per-PE decoders, shorter critical path):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 9: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth on the activation with\n// the digit decode hoisted to the array input, control skew (no eastward\n// chains), and row-broadcast combinational product injection.\n//\n// Schedule: A row m must be multiplied by PE(i,j) at cycle m+i+j.  The\n// partial-sum chain provides the per-row stage delay, so every PE of row i\n// can compute its product COMBINATIONALLY at cycle m+i from the row-shared\n// (broadcast) skewed activation and inject it into its column's psum chain\n// in the same cycle.  This removes the eastward shift chains entirely.\n//\n// The radix-4 Booth digit controls (sel, neg, two per digit) depend only on\n// the activation, so they are decoded ONCE per row at the array input\n// (8 decoders instead of 64) and skewed as 12-bit vectors; each row's\n// controls are broadcast to all its PEs.  Inside a PE the row generation is\n// a stationary-weight mux plus one XOR and one AND.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [11:0]\t  c_row,      // Booth digit controls (row broadcast)\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0\n   wire [3:0] sel = {c_row[11], c_row[8], c_row[5], c_row[2]};\n   wire [3:0] neg = {c_row[10], c_row[7], c_row[4], c_row[1]};\n   wire [3:0] two = {c_row[9],  c_row[6], c_row[3], c_row[0]};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (-1)^neg_i * (two_i ? 2w : w) << (2*i),\n   // as 10-bit signed magnitudes sign-extended to IW bits.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n            wire xk = (k == 0) ? (two[i] ? 1'b0 : w[0]) :\n\t\t      ((k >= 1) && (k <= 7)) ?\n\t\t\t\t\t(two[i] ? w[k-1] : w[k]) :\n\t\t      w[DW-1];\t// k == 8 or 9: sign bit\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] & (neg[i] ^ xk);\n         end\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){rp[9]}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // negation correction bits (+1) at columns 0, 2, 4, 6\n   wire [3:0] corr;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_corr\n         assign corr[i] = sel[i] & neg[i];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // absorb corr[0..2] into unused low slots of rows 1..3; corr[3] stands alone\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1c = brow[1] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr[0]});\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2c = brow[2] | ({{(IW-2){1'b0}}, corr[1]} << 2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3c = brow[3] | ({{(IW-3){1'b0}}, corr[2]} << 4);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c3v = {{(IW-7){1'b0}}, corr[3]} << 6;\n\n   // incoming carry-save row (g is stored unshifted)\n   wire [IW-1:0] crow = {g_north, 1'b0};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Compression of 7 rows (B0, B1', B2', B3', corr3, S, C) down to 2:\n   // 5 compressors, 4 levels (minimum for 7 rows with 3:2 compressors).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0, u0g, u1, u1g;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_a (.x(brow[0]), .y(b1c),  .z(b2c),   .s(u0), .g(u0g));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_b (.x(b3c),     .y(c3v),  .z(s_north), .s(u1), .g(u1g));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] v0, v1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_c (.x(u0), .y({u0g[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(u1),\n\t\t       .s(v0), .g(v1));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] w0r, w1r;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_d (.x(v0), .y({v1[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z({u1g[IW-2:0], 1'b0}),\n\t\t       .s(w0r), .g(w1r));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] f0, f1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_e (.x(w0r), .y({w1r[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(crow),\n\t\t       .s(f0), .g(f1));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         s_south <= f0;\n         g_south <= f1[IW-2:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*12-1:0]\tc_row,    // per-row Booth digit controls (broadcast)\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\ts_bot,\n    output [N*(IW-1)-1:0] g_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-2:0] g_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign g_v[0][j] = {(IW-1){1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign s_bot[j*IW +: IW] = s_v[N][j];\n         assign g_bot[j*(IW-1) +: (IW-1)] = g_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk      (clk),\n               .ld       (ld),\n               .en       (en),\n               .w_north  (w_v[i][j]),\n               .c_row    (c_row[i*12 +: 12]),\n               .s_north  (s_v[i][j]),\n               .g_north  (g_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south  (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .s_south  (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .g_south  (g_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Booth digit decode, once per row, on the (validity-qualified) input\n   // activation; decode(0) == 0, so a low a_valid injects all-zero controls\n   // (no product).  The controls are then skewed: row i is delayed i cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*12-1:0] c_row;\n   genvar\t   q, d, i;\n   generate\n      for (q = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n         wire [DW-1:0] aq = a_valid ? a_data[q*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         reg [11:0] sr [0:q];\t// sr[0] = decoded value (no delay)\n         always @* begin\n            for (d = 0; d < 4; d = d + 1) begin : g_d\n               wire hi  = aq[2*d+1];\n               wire mid = aq[2*d];\n               wire lo  = (d == 0) ? 1'b0 : aq[2*d-1];\n               sr[0][3*d + 2] = (hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo);\n               sr[0][3*d + 1] = hi & ~(mid & lo);\n               sr[0][3*d]     = (hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo);\n            end\n         end\n         // skew chain (row q: q delays); row 0 has none\n         if (q > 0) begin : g_skew\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[1] <= sr[0];\n                  for (k = 2; Design8 passes (2.4987M) — the marginal gain suggests control-chains ≈ activation-chains in cost. The real win: in design8 the **decoder sits on the critical path** (skew FF → decode → row-gen → CSA tree). I'll decode directly from `a_data` at the input, qualify with `a_valid`, and skew the **12-bit controls** instead of the raw activations — moving the decode off the register-to-register path entirely:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 10: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth with the digit decode\n// at the array INPUT (before the skew registers), 12-bit control chains.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19. 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Booth digit decode, once per row, directly on a_data //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*12-1:0] c_row;\n   genvar\t   q, d;\n   generate\n      for (q = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n wire lo  = (d == 0) ? 1'b0 : aq[2*d-1];\n            assign dec[3*d + 2] = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo));\n            assign dec[3*d + 1] = a_valid & (hi & ~(mid & lo));\n            assign dec[3*d]     = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo));\n         end\n         if (q == 0) begin : g_sk0\n            assign c_row[q*12 +: 12] = dec;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skn\n [ASSISTANT]
Design10 hits **2.31M** (delay 518). Now a bigger structural idea: with the Booth decode hoisted to the input, the control chains east are 12 bits wide. But if I instead **broadcast** the controls within each row and delay each row's controls by i+7 cycles, every column's bottom result lands exactly at cycle m+15 — **zero de-skew FFs** and no eastward chains at all (saves ~630 FFs). The schedule math: PE(i,j) must process row m at m+τ_i with τ_i = τ_0+i and τ_0+8 = 15 → τ_i = i+7 exactly.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 11: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth on the activation,\n// decode at the array input, row-broadcast controls with (i+7) skew, and\n// zero output de-skew.\n//\n// Schedule: PE(i,j) must multiply A row m at compute cycle m + tau_i with\n// tau_i = tau_0 + i (psum-chain consistency) and the column-bottom result\n// (registered in PE(7,j)) present during cycle m + tau_7 + 1 = m + 15\n// (= the mandated c_valid cycle) forces tau_0 = 7, i.e. tau_i = i + 7.\n// So each row's Booth digit controls are delayed by i+7 cycles and\n// BROADCAST to all 8 PEs of the row (no eastward chains, no de-skew\n// chains).  The bottom partial sums are converted to binary by one 19-bit\n// CPA per column straight to the output.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17 needs 19 signed bits: exact mod 2^19.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [11:0]\t  c_row,      // Booth digit controls (row broadcast)\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0\n   wire [3:0] sel = {c_row[11], c_row[8], c_row[5], c_row[2]};\n   wire [3:0] neg = {c_row[10], c_row[7], c_row[4], c_row[1]};\n   wire [3:0] two = {c_row[9],  c_row[6], c_row[3], c_row[0]};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (-1)^neg_i * (two_i ? 2w : w) << (2*i),\n   // as 10-bit signed magnitudes sign-extended to IW bits.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n            wire xk = (k == 0) ? (two[i] ? 1'b0 : w[0]) :\n\t\t      ((k >= 1) && (k <= 7)) ?\n\t\t\t\t\t(two[i] ? w[k-1] : w[k]) :\n\t\t      w[DW-1];\t// k == 8 or 9: sign bit\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] & (neg[i] ^ xk);\n         end\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){rp[9]}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // negation correction bits (+1) at columns 0, 2, 4, 6\n   wire [3:0] corr;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_corr\n         assign corr[i] = sel[i] & neg[i];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // absorb corr[0..2] into unused low slots of rows 1..3; corr[3] stands alone\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1c = brow[1] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr[0]});\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2c = brow[2] | ({{(IW-2){1'b0}}, corr[1]} << 2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3c = brow[3] | ({{(IW-3){1'b0}}, corr[2]} << (g_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Booth digit decode, //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*12-1:0] c_row;\n   genvar\t   q, d;\n   generate\n      for (q = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n [ASSISTANT]
The failure taught me something crucial: the golden's per-PE injection schedule is exactly τ(i,j)=i+j, and **mid-stream weight reloads make products before/after the reload see different weights** — so any deviation from that schedule (like row-broadcast) changes results. Design10 (2.31M) is my best. Next: move the Booth decode to the array input (before the skew registers), so it's off the register-to-register path, while keeping the exact τ(i,j)=i+j schedule:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 13: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth with the digit decode\n// at the array INPUT (before the skew registers) -> decode off the\n// register-to-register critical path.  Schedule identical to the golden:\n// PE(i,j) multiplies A row m at compute cycle m+i+j.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// The per-row Booth digit controls depend only on the activation, so they\n// are decoded ONCE per row directly on a_data (decode(0) = 0, so\n// AND-qualifying with a_valid injects zeros when idle), skewed by i cycles\n// (row i), and piped east through the array (one hop per cycle) so PE(i,j)\n// gets row m's controls at cycle m+i+j - exactly the golden schedule.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [11:0]\t  c_west,     // Booth digit controls in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [11:0]\t  c_east,     // Booth digit controls out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0\n   wire [3:0] sel = {c_west[11], c_west[8], c_west[5], c_west[2]};\n   wire [3:0] neg = {c_west[10], c_west[7], c_west[4], c_west[1]};\n   wire [3:0] two = {c_west[9],  c_west[6], c_west[3], c_west[0]};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (-1)^neg_i * (two_i ? 2w : w) << (2*i),\n   // as 10-bit signed magnitudes sign-extended to IW bits.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n            wire xk = (k == 0) ? (two[i] ? 1'b0 : w[0]) :\n\t\t      ((k >= 1) && (k <= 7)) ?\n\t\t\t\t\t(two[i] ? w[k-1] : w[k]) :\n\t\t      w[DW-1];\t// k == 8 or 9: sign bit\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] & (neg[i] ^ xk);\n         end\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){rp[9]}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // negation correction bits (+1) at columns 0, 2, 4, 6\n   wire [3:0] corr;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_corr\n         assign corr[i] = sel[i] & neg[i];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // absorb corr[0..2] into unused low slots of rows 1..3; corr[3] stands alone\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1c = brow[1] | ({{(IW-1){1'b0}}, corr[0]});\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2c = brow[2] | ({{(IW-2){1'b0}}, corr[1]} << 2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3c = brow[3] | ({{(IW-3){1'b0}}, corr[2]} << //##########################################################################\n   // Compression of 7 rows (B0, B1', B2', B3', corr3, S, C) down to 2:\n   // 5 compressors, 4 levels (minimum for 7 rows #(.W(IW)) u_e (.x(w0r), .y({w1r[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(crow),\n\t\t       .s(f0), .g(f1));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         c_east  <= c_west;\n         s_south <= f0;\n         g_south <= f1[IW-2:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n      assign s_bot[j*IW +: IW] = s_v[N][j];\n         assign g_bot[j*(IW-1) +: (IW-1)] = g_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         assign c_h[i][0] = endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\tld = w_valid;\n   wire\ten = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Booth digit decode, once per row, directly on a_data (decode(0) = 0,\n   // so AND-qualifying with a_valid injects zero controls when idle).\n   // The CONTROLS are then skewed: row i is delayed i compute cycles, and\n   // piped east through the array (PE(i,j) at cycle m+i+j - golden schedule).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*12-1:0] c_row;\n   genvar\t   q, d;\n   generate\n      for (q = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n         wire [DW-1:0] aq = a_data[q*DW +: DW];\n         wire [11:0]   dec;\n         for (d = 0; d < 4; d = d + 1) begin : g_d\n            wire hi  = aq[2*d+1];\n            wire mid = aq[2*d];\n            wire lo  = (d == 0) ? 1'b0 : aq[2*d-1];\n            assign dec[3*d + 2] = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo));\n            assign dec[3*d + 1] = a_valid & (hi & ~(mid & lo));\n            assign dec[3*d]     = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo));\n         end\n         if (q == 0) begin : g_sk0\n            assign c_row[q*12 +: 12] = {{(ACCW-IW){tf[IW-1]}}, tf};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if [ASSISTANT]
Excellent — design13 ties design10 (2.31M), confirming the decode placement is now off-path; the critical path is rowgen + 4-level CSA. Next big win: **eliminate the correction-bit row** by registering exact ±w (and ±2w by shift) at load time — this removes the negation "+1" rows entirely, cutting the CSA tree from 7 rows/4 levels to **6 rows/3 levels** (fewer compressors AND less depth):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 14: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth with exact +/-w\n// vectors registered at load time (no correction rows) + input decode.\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Radix-4 Booth on the ACTIVATION: a*w = sum_i d_i * w * 4^i, digits d_i\n// in {-2..2} decoded once per row at the array input from a_data (decode(0)\n// = 0, so AND-qualifying with a_valid injects zeros when idle); the 12-bit\n// controls are skewed by i cycles (row i) and piped east, so PE(i,j)\n// multiplies A row m at cycle m+i+j (the golden schedule).\n//\n// Each PE registers, at load time, the exact 10-bit vectors +w and -w\n// (sign-extended).  Product row i is then a pure mux:\n//   row_i = sel_i * (neg_i ? (two_i ? (-w)<<1 : -w) : (two_i ? w<<1 : w))\n//                                          << (2*i),\n// with NO negation correction bits, so the compression tree handles only\n// SIX rows (4 Booth rows + S + C): 4 compressors, 3 levels.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [11:0]\t  c_west,     // Booth digit controls in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [11:0]\t  c_east,     // Booth digit controls out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   // +w / -w as 10-bit signed vectors, registered at load time\n   wire [9:0] vpp = {w_north[DW-1], w_north[DW-1], w_north};\n   reg [9:0] vp, vn;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         vp <= vpp;\n         vn <= ~vpp + 10'b1;\t// exact -w\n      end\n   end\n   assign w_south = w_north;\t// weight pass-through (no per-PE weight reg)\n\n   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0\n   wire [3:0] sel = {c_west[11], c_west[8], c_west[5], c_west[2]};\n   wire [3:0] neg = {c_west[10], c_west[7], c_west[4], c_west[1]};\n   wire [3:0] two = {c_west[9],  c_west[6], c_west[3], c_west[0]};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (neg_i ? -(two_i ? 2w : w)\n   //                                      : (two_i ? 2w : w)) << (2*i),\n   // as exact 10-bit signed values (from the registered +/-w vectors),\n   // sign-extended to IW bits.  No correction bits.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n            wire pk = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : vp[k-1];   // (2w)[k],   +w family\n            wire nk = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : vn[k-1];   // (-2w)[k], -w family\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] &\n\t\t\t   (neg[i] ? (two[i] ? nk : vn[k])\n\t\t\t\t   : (two[i] ? pk : vp[k]));\n         end\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){rp[9]}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // incoming carry-save row (g is stored unshifted)\n   wire [IW-1:0] crow = {g_north, 1'b0};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Compression of 6 rows (B0..B3, S, C) down to 2: 4 compressors, 3 levels.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] s1, g1, s2, g2;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_a (.x(brow[0]), .y(brow[1]), .z(brow[2]), .s(s1), .g(g1));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_b (.x(brow[3]), .y(s_north), .z(crow),    .s(s2), .g(g2));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s3, g3;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_c (.x(s1), .y({g1[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(s2), .s(s3), .g(g3));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] f0, f1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_d (.x(g2), .y(s3), .z({g3[IW-2:0], 1'b0}),\n\t\t       .s(f0), .g(f1));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (en) begin\n         c_east  <= c_west;\n         s_south <= f0;\n         g_south <= f1[IW-2:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*12-1:0]\tc_left,   // per-row Booth digit controls\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\ts_bot,\n    output [N*(IW-1)-1:0] g_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [12-1:0] c_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-2:0] g_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign g_v[0][j] = {(IW-1){1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign s_bot[j*IW +: IW] = s_v[N][j];\n         assign g_bot[j*(IW-1) +: (IW-1)] = g_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n         wire [DW-1:0] aq = a_data[q*DW +: DW];\n         wire [11:0]   dec;\n         for (d = 0; d < 4; d = d + 1) begin : g_d\n            wire hi  = aq[2*d+1];\n            wire mid = aq[2*d];\n            wire lo  = (d == 0) ? 1'b0 : aq[2*d-1];\n            assign dec[3*d + 2] = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo));\n            assign dec[3*d + 1] = a_valid & (hi & ~(mid & lo));\n            assign dec[3*d]     = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo));\n         end\n         if (q == 0) begin : g_sk0\n            assign c_row[q*12 +: 12] = dec;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skn\n            reg [11:0] sr [0:q-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= dec;\n                  for (k = 1; module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter IW = 19)	 // carry-save accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [11:0]	  c_west,     // Booth digit controls in from west
    input [IW-1:0]	  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above
    input [IW-2:0]	  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [11:0]	  c_east,     // Booth digit controls out to east
    output reg [IW-1:0]	  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below
    output reg [IW-2:0]	  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below
    );

   // +w / -w as 10-bit signed vectors, registered at load time (from
   // w_north, so they always match the registered weight w, even on
   // partial reloads)
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   wire [9:0] vpp = {w_north[DW-1], w_north[DW-1], w_north};
   reg [9:0] vp, vn;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (ld) begin
         w  <= w_north;
         vp <= vpp;
         vn <= ~vpp + 10'b1;	// exact -w
      end
   end
   assign w_south = w;		// stored weight out to PE below

   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0
   wire [3:0] sel = {c_west[11], c_west[8], c_west[5], c_west[2]};

   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (neg_i ? -(two_i ? 2w : w)

[ASSISTANT]
Design14 passes at **2.227M**. Now a major area cut: replace the 532-FF de-skew chains with **capture registers** (column j's bottom result appears at cycle m+8+j; c_valid at m+15; the next capture is ≥8 cycles later — so for j≤6 a single register per column suffices, and j=7 needs none since its result arrives exactly at m+15). Also drop the per-PE weight register (w_south = vp[7:0]):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\n// Variant 15: carry-save accumulation + radix-4 Booth with exact +/-w\n// vectors registered at load (no correction rows) + input decode +\n// capture-register output alignment (no de-skew shift chains).\n//\n// Partial sums flow down each column in carry-save (redundant) form:\n// (S, G) with S + (G << 1) == psum (mod 2^IW), IW = 19.  G's bit 0 is\n// structurally always 0, so only G[IW-2:0] is registered.\n// Max |psum| = 8 * 128 * 128 = 2^17, which needs 19 signed bits, so all\n// arithmetic is exact mod 2^19.\n//\n// Schedule (identical to the golden array): PE(i,j) multiplies A row m at\n// compute cycle m+i+j, so column j's complete row-m result is registered at\n// PE(7,j) at the end of cycle m+7+j, i.e. available during cycle m+8+j,\n// while c_valid for row m is at m+15.  Column 7's result is therefore\n// presented directly (combinational CPA at the output), and columns 0..6\n// each capture their converted result into ONE register when a_valid\n// delayed by 8+j cycles is high; that register provably holds row m's value\n// during cycle m+15 (next capture of column j is 8 cycles later, and for\n// j<=6 the capture happens at or before m+14, i.e. before the read cycle).\n//\n// Each PE registers +w / -w (10-bit signed) at load time; w_south is simply\n// vp[7:0], so no separate weight register is needed.  Product row i is a\n// pure mux over the four registered magnitudes, with NO negation correction\n// bits, so the compression tree handles only SIX rows (4 Booth rows + S + C):\n// 4 compressors, 3 levels.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// 3:2 carry-save compressor: s + (g << 1) == x + y + z (mod 2^W)\nmodule csa3\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    input [W-1:0] z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] g);\t\t// unshifted carry\n\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign g = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)\t // carry-save accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [11:0]\t  c_west,     // Booth digit controls in from west\n    input [IW-1:0]\t  s_north,    // partial sum (sum vector) from above\n    input [IW-2:0]\t  g_north,    // partial sum (carry vector) from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [11:0]\t  c_east,     // Booth digit controls out to east\n    output reg [IW-1:0]\t  s_south,    // partial sum (sum vector) out to below\n    output reg [IW-2:0]\t  g_south     // partial sum (carry vector) out to below\n    );\n\n   // +w / -w as 10-bit signed vectors, registered at load time (from\n   // w_north, so they always match the registered weight, even on partial\n   // reloads).  The stored weight itself is vp[7:0].\n   wire [9:0] vpp = {w_north[DW-1], w_north[DW-1], w_north};\n   reg [9:0] vp, vn;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         vp <= vpp;\n         vn <= ~vpp + 10'b1;\t// exact -w\n      end\n   end\n   assign w_south = vp[DW-1:0];\n\n   // per-digit controls: {sel_i, neg_i, two_i} for i = 3..0\n   wire [3:0] sel = {c_west[11], c_west[8], c_west[5], c_west[2]};\n   wire [3:0] neg = {c_west[10], c_west[7], c_west[4], c_west[1]};\n   wire [3:0] two = {c_west[9],  c_west[6], c_west[3], c_west[0]};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Product rows: row i = sel_i * (neg_i ? -(two_i ? 2w : w)\n   //                                      : (two_i ? 2w : w)) << (2*i),\n   // as exact 10-bit signed values (from the registered +/-w vectors),\n   // sign-extended to IW bits.  No correction bits.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] brow [0:3];\n   genvar i, k;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         wire [9:0] rp;\n         for (k = 0; k < 10; k = k + 1) begin : g_rp\n            wire pk = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : vp[k-1];   // (2w)[k],   +w family\n            wire nk = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : vn[k-1];   // (-2w)[k], -w family\n            assign rp[k] = sel[i] &\n\t\t\t   (neg[i] ? (two[i] ? nk : vn[k])\n\t\t\t\t   : (two[i] ? pk : vp[k]));\n         end\n         assign brow[i] = ({{(IW-10){rp[9]}}, rp} << (2*i));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // incoming carry-save row (g is stored unshifted)\n   wire [IW-1:0] crow = {g_north, 1'b0};\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Compression of 6 rows (B0..B3, S, C) down to 2: 4 compressors, 3 levels.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] s1, g1, s2, g2;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_a (.x(brow[0]), .y(brow[1]), .z(brow[2]), .s(s1), .g(g1));\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_b (.x(brow[3]), .y(s_north), .z(crow),    .s(s2), .g(g2));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] s3, g3;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_c (.x(s1), .y({g1[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .z(s2), .s(s3), .g(g3));\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] f0, f1;\n   csa3 #(.W(IW)) u_d (.x({g2[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .y(s3),\n\t\t       .z({g3[IW-2:0], 1'b0}), .s(f0), .g(f1));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (en) begin\n         c_east  <= c_west;\n         s_south <= f0;\n         g_south <= f1[IW-2:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*12-1:0]\tc_left,   // per-row Booth digit controls\n    output [N*IW-1:0]\ts_bot,\n    output [N*(IW-1)-1:0] g_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [12-1:0] c_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-2:0] g_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; q < N; q = q + 1) begin : g_dec\n         wire [DW-1:0] aq = a_data[q*DW +: DW];\n         wire [11:0]   dec;\n         for (d = 0; d < 4; d = d + 1) begin : g_d\n            wire hi  = aq[2*d+1];\n            wire mid = aq[2*d];\n            wire lo  = (d == 0) ? 1'b0 : aq[2*d-1];\n            assign dec[3*d + 2] = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) | (mid ^ lo));\n            assign dec[3*d + 1] = a_valid & (hi & ~(mid & lo));\n            assign dec[3*d]     = a_valid & ((hi ^ mid) & ~(mid ^ lo));\n         end\n         if (q == 0) begin : g_sk0\n            assign c_row[q*12 +: 12] = dec;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skn\n            reg [11:0] sr [0:q-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= dec;\n                  for (k = 1; # Design Summary — `tpu` (weight-stationary 8×8 MXU)

**Final best: `design14.v` — PASS, cost 2,226,857** (vs. starting point 4,624,409, a 2.08× improvement). Area 4588, delay 485 ps (slack −5.7 ps).

## 1. Approaches tried (in order)

| File | Idea | Cost | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| starting_point.v | Original logikbench RTL | 4.62M | PASS |
| design1.v | Internal accumulator 32→19 bits (max \|Σ·a·b\| = 8·128·128 = 2^17 needs exactly 19 signed bits), sign-extend only at output | 3.24M | PASS |
| design2.v | Carry-save (redundant) psum chain: raw partial-product rows + 13-CSA tree; CPA only once per column after de-skew | 2.83M | PASS |
| design3.v | + radix-4 Booth (5 CSAs, 4 levels) | 2.62M | PASS |
| design5.v | + unshifted carry vector (18 stored bits, bit 0 structurally 0), binary de-skew, reset only on valid pipe | 2.50M | PASS |
| design8/10/13.v | Booth digit decode hoisted out of the 64 PEs — 12-bit control chains (sel/neg/two), decode at array input before skew | 2.31M | PASS |
| design14.v | + exact ±w registered at load time → **no correction-bit rows**, 4 CSAs / 3 levels | **2.23M** | PASS |

## 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **Narrowing the accumulator 32→19 bits** (pure math insight; −30% cost, zero risk).
2. **Carry-save psum chains** — removing the 19-bit CPA from every PE was the single biggest structural win; conversion happens once per column.
3. **Radix-4 Booth on the activation with decode hoisted to the array input**: product rows depend on the activation only through 12 control bits (sel/neg/two per digit), so 8 decoders replace 64 and the decode leaves the PE critical path.
4. **Registering exact ±w at load** — Booth's "+1" correction rows vanish, shrinking the CSA tree from 7 rows/4 levels to 6 rows/3 levels (both area and delay).

## 3. What failed / regressed
- **IW=18** is arithmetically impossible: max positive sum +131072 = 2^17 needs 19 signed bits.
- **Booth-on-weight (design7)**: pre-decoding w is legal but control chains interact badly with mid-stream reloads and row-broadcast variants violate the exact τ(i,j)=i+j injection schedule the golden trace enforces (mid-drain weight reload vectors catch any deviation).
- **Row-broadcast capture scheme (design11/15)**: replacing de-skew chains with capture registers failed on multi-tile/back-to-back streams (values stale by one row) — the de-skew chain in design14 is the correct cycle-accurate structure. Never fully recovered the alignment proof.
- Recurring Verilog pitfalls that cost debugging turns: 1-bit shift operands losing bits, `wire` declarations inside procedural loops, `D==0` empty array ranges, and mis-placed carry-shift in a hand-built CSA tree.

## 4. Lessons learned
- The golden trace's *stall + reload semantics* pin the per-PE schedule exactly (τ=i+j); only optimizations preserving it are viable. Verify schedule-equivalence before building.
- Narrow datapaths to the provable dynamic range first — cheapest large win.
- In systolic arrays, attack the repeating cell's critical path (redundant arithmetic, fewer compression levels) rather than I/O plumbing, which is already minimal.
- When hand-crafting CSA trees, double-check every carry input is shifted and every row count is complete — one wrong wire produces distinctive "÷8" or all-ones symptoms that pinpoint the bug.
- Next time: try `target_delay` sweeps only after RTL structure is settled; structure dominated every result here (300/500/850 ps targets were nearly inert).
